**Pinned report source:** [OpenTUI session.zig](https://github.com/anomalyco/opentui/blob/738067f47e940dcec3f5aab74868908574335c6c/packages/native/src/session.zig), `writeProgramStatus:1163-1176`, validation `754-794`, struct `185-198`, bounds `234-246`, `Session.control:707-751`, publish `1094-1121`. Supplied exact-pin research defines `ESC]7501;state=<enum>` with fixed optional order `:id=`, `:kind=`, `:progress=`, `:app=`, `:title=`, `:msg=`, then ST only. States are idle/working/done/blocked/error/clear. Kind permission/question/auth requires blocked. Progress 0..100 requires working/blocked. Clear forbids app/title/msg. ID has at most 128 bytes and eight nonempty slash segments, each 1..32 ASCII `[A-Za-z0-9_.+-]`. App has 1..32 bytes of that charset. Title/message bounds are 192/2048 UTF-8 bytes, valid UTF-8 without C0/DEL/C1, emitted as base64. Full report is at most 3220 bytes. Reports are non-cell metadata, emitted as standalone atomic packets in active phase, not screen/cursor/palette operations. These facts do not guarantee global renderer placement or captured field values. Query `?` is separate.

The complete token exactly matches one source-conformant idle report with app present and no other fields, using the known application identifier. Equality is over the complete operand and ST framing, not its length or OSC number. Raw captured payload is not exported. Implement only that exact finite variant, with no base64 decoding or generic report parser. All other states, fields, values, orders, encodings and terminators stay refused. Require alternate screen active, before the first completed update, outside an update. These narrower placement restrictions are observer policy based on this prefix, not source guarantees. Preserve all prior refusal, glyph, index/RGB, screen, cursor, generation, reset and prefix rules.

OSC66 probe paint is tainted until a full clear or alternate-screen generation. Its unknown advance invalidates cursor position. Unknown initial coordinates survive save/restore. A second save is a persistent register replacement, not a no-op or a stack that needs balancing. Restore without a matching generation refuses. Enter/exit1049 clears/discards the projected generation, exit cannot preserve a stale success. Only completed, nonnested, matched synchronized updates can return a frame. Lone source scalars advance one cell. Any unknown scalar, combining mark, VS16 or ZWJ refuses rather than being skipped. The source observer rejects unsynchronized paint and geometry overflow, with no implied wrapping. Unsupported terminal behaviors intentionally remain unsupported.

`rendererBuildCapture` recomputes F, N and actual six-surface lengths from complete admitted buffers before path encoding/concatenation. **Outer export boundary:** `rendererTransferDetails` admits each stopped-container `docker cp <owned-container>:/run/proceed/evidence/<fixed-name> -` TAR into memory with `maxBuffer = remaining detail bytes + 4096` before receiving/parsing. Each envelope must have exactly the expected ordinary member, valid size/checksum, no link/prefix/extension and zero padding/end blocks. Payload length is charged against the aggregate remaining 262144-byte detail budget, including the actual marker bytes, even extra JSON whitespace. The marker gate precedes any other detail transfer. No detail is written locally until all eight members pass. No filesystem-target unrestricted Docker copy remains in renderer mode. The separately closed diagnostic has an 8192-byte payload and 4096-byte TAR-overhead ceiling before parsing/retention. Diagnostic fallback reuses an already admitted diagnostic rather than rereading it, or makes the existing single bounded attempt if none was attempted. Refusal cannot trigger raw/detail fallback or retry. TAR overhead is transport-only, at most 4096 bytes/member, not detail content or an RSS claim.

**Exact remaining parser gap and minimal future calibration brief:** styled parsing scans the cumulative transcript and rejects any non-ASCII cell, including ordinary Unicode UI decoration before the picker. Consequently, the current ASCII subset can reject the whole normal UI even if the picker identity itself is ASCII. It does not ignore arbitrary Unicode or claim host compatibility. ### NH-FAILURE-RETENTION-001 attempt 1 - test engineering correction

Assigned by coordination. Previous source permits a complete failure scan only after graceful wrapper close, then requests termination after the scan opportunity. This can lose safe operands and every detailed observation when Ctrl+C merely dismisses a dialog. This is a source-established defect, not a reconstructed cause of attempt 4.

- Affected files: `tests/support/harness-composition-{inner,readiness,retention}.mjs`, `tests/support/harness-linux-{runner,plan}.mjs`, `tests/harness-{failure-retention,composition-preparation}.test.mjs` and this task record. No application, dependency or contract changes.
- Criteria: typed phase deadline distinct from assertion and unlisted IO. Freeze the full closed scalar guard projection, failure phase and pre-cleanup key counts at catch time. Persist the validated failure before cleanup begins. Same-directory replacement preserves the earlier record if the final write/commit fails. Keep later cleanup-entry observations separate. Never serialize arbitrary errors, messages, stacks, raw frames, paths or public IDs into ungated diagnostics.
- One normal exit request at most. A fake dialog dismissal is not exit. Record exit event, stream close and requested termination separately. Owned process-group termination precedes capture admission. PID-baseline comparison conservatively refuses scanning if any new container process remains. It is not actual helper-absence or graceful product Cleanup proof.
- Freeze the complete `transportFixtures` secret set before execution. Establish the empty wire observer stream before launch rather than invent missing capture bytes later. After controls close, require wrapper close, no new remaining PID, all six required buffers, diagnostics presence and bounded captures. Snapshot exact detailed observations and exports before scanning. Both ordinary and terminated paths use the real `scanRequiredCaptures` mechanic inside the private container. Forced termination alone never creates completeness or a PASS marker. Missing, leaking, incomplete, overflowing or failed captures refuse all detailed export.
- The outer runner revalidates the closed diagnostic before retention and removes its private quarantine file. Scan markers must name exactly all six composition surfaces. The marker is written only after the immutable scan and detailed artifact writes. Outer progress appends phase history, keeps its first observed container exit through teardown/complete, and separates inner progress to avoid overwriting it.
- Native controls: `tests/harness-failure-retention.test.mjs` covers picker assertion, typed deadline, unlisted IO, one Ctrl+C leaving a fake host alive, termination then complete frozen captures, overflow, missing file-buffer, authority marker, incomplete/missing scan refusal, earlier-exit preservation and closed safe projection. These are scripted lifecycle controls, not actual exit/privacy/runtime proof.

## Boundary Verification and combined review trigger

- **Real external boundary - OpenCode:** reuse pinned design source grounding for OpenCode/actual SDK **2.2.5**, commit `9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21`. Qualify TUI-only `setup`/`Cleanup`, `ui.dialog.select`/`prompt`/`show`, explicit selection from `data.session.list()` with title and exact ID, `data.session.get` verification and transport `_meta["ai.opencode/sessionID"]`. `PanelInput.sessionID` is not a required mounting point. A command dispatch opens UI only. Service form reply/RPC Basic auth cannot choose. MCP `accept` is submission, not domain approval. MCP references remain versioned 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25, not an invented native human-attestation API. Actual-host rendered interaction, ordinary-API attacks and shared-connection routing are required proof, not mocks.
- **Real secure local wire:** exact origin `https://127.0.0.1:<configured-port>` with startup's protected-instance TLS-PSK verification. `Authorization: Bearer <operator-credential>` on `POST /v1/harness/enrollments`, `/v1/harness/pending`, `/v1/harness/presentations/{presentation_id}`, `/v1/harness/decisions`. Exact payload/result/error names come from design section 2's table, without renaming or new schemas. Verify that table over the actual protected broker socket and actual TUI runtime. No redirect/proxy/downgrade or owner/control substitution. Fake-broker denial must happen before secret disclosure.
- **Real external boundary - Telegram:** existing fixed `https://api.telegram.org/bot<token>/<method>` transport from `src/telegram/client.js`. Preserve private paired sender/chat checks. New `/harness <enrollment_id>`, `/harness_revoke <key_id>` and `he:<token>`/`hx:<token>` are enrollment/revocation, not request decisions. Controlled fixtures establish parser/state rules only. Task 4.4 requires actual Telegram proof for the changed flow with scoped access, never token-bearing URLs in evidence.
- **Normative cross-component contracts:** design sections 2-3 own exact optional `harness_route`, `harness_channel`, `SESSION_BUSY`, enrollment/presentation IDs, typed choices and durable binding. Review names, payloads, compatibility and v2/v3 reader safety. Merged `src/broker/portable-server.js` implements TLS-PSK with exact selector/cipher and owner/control roles, but no harness routes. `tests/support/automatic-startup.mjs` supplies an independent real secure exchange. Its 1000 ms diagnostic exchange budget is stricter than the product's 10000 ms ceiling and is not proof of the full product deadline. Purely private seams need no extra block/review or invented wire URL.
- **Decomposition checklist:** (1) YES, enrollment/route/v3 state feeds later proof. (2) YES, upgrade/revocation/resolution mutate clean pending state. (3) YES, observers and live dialogs must exist before choices/events. (4) YES, authority, routing and installed-first-use proofs span groups. (5) YES, enrollment setup and decision proof are ordered. Therefore task 1.1 requests one combined decomposition/boundary/test-only review before readiness. Review the synthetic credential and protocol assertions in `tests/harness-checkpoint.test.mjs` with this block. They define no host SDK types or private production interface.
- **Proof hygiene:** each case owns separate disposable state or explicitly retains the same request across restart. Arm observation before effects. Never resolve a shared baseline request in setup, confuse fixture confirmation with live receipt, or reuse a dialog promise as authority. Group 2's minimal real production path enables its own proof without assuming group 3 exists. Group 3 hardening must revalidate any changed qualification inputs.

### Phase B preparation checkpoint - fixture and driver prerequisites only

This is the authorized coherent preparation checkpoint, not completion of task 1.4 or permission to begin production TUI work. Founder-visible success remains a real operator choice for the exact originating request without ordinary API self-approval. These files only make later platform proof possible. No actual host, service, model, provider, Telegram or key was launched or loaded.

- `tests/fixtures/harness-platform/tui.ts` is a genuine `Plugin.define` fixture importing `@opencode/plugin/tui`. Actual `setup` registers a client-local keyboard command, returns Cleanup, and uses real `ui.dialog.select` and `prompt`. Reject is initially selected. Esc records dismissal only. Respond uses a second text interaction. Cleanup fences late completions. It has no broker client, secret loading, service decision RPC, captured callback test or mock host context. Fixture JSONL records contain synthetic UI choices only. A setup/select-requested event is explicitly not rendered readiness.
- `tests/support/harness-host-plan.mjs` owns the minimal MSYS candidate arguments, fresh child environment and planned keyboard sequence. `tests/harness-host-preparation.test.mjs` tests those pure preparation rules without importing the fixture or any production source. The transcript predicate is a minimal readiness aid, not a terminal emulator or evidence that keyboard input works.
- `tests/run-harness-host.mjs --preflight` is the maintained NO-LAUNCH prerequisite command. It checks exact declared/installed development dependency versions, reports remaining gates and always exits 2 (BLOCKED). It does not create run state, spawn OpenCode or qualify a runtime. A live managed runner is deliberately not implemented by this checkpoint. Do not reinterpret this command as actual host execution.

**Pinned source isolation controls:** operational discovery supplied official commit `9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21`, including `util/global-roots.ts`, `global.ts`, `core/credential.ts`, `cli/database-path.ts`, `cli/server-process.ts`, migration `20260805200742_import_legacy_credentials.ts`, `core/config/discovery.ts` and TUI `app.tsx`. These facts are source evidence, not runtime proof:

- Set `XDG_DATA_HOME`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME`, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, `XDG_STATE_HOME`, `TEMP` and `TMP` before spawning/importing the host. Set `OPENCODE_TEST_HOME` and `USERPROFILE` to the run home. The preparation environment also gives MSYS `HOME` and `TMPDIR` that same owned namespace. Never inherit user home/config/data defaults.
- `OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` controls service configuration, not CLI configuration. Redirect it separately and stage only the fixture's `cli.json` under `<XDG_CONFIG_HOME>/opencode/`. Redirect Windows `ProgramData` because system config discovery otherwise reads the real system directory. Global `.claude` and `.agents` follow the redirected home.
- Set exactly `OPENCODE_CONFIG_PROJECT_DISABLE=1`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_MODELS_FETCH=1`, `OPENCODE_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATE=1`. Do not invent extra discovery or network-disable variables. The fresh allowlist excludes inherited service passwords, provider keys, askpass, telemetry, preload, plugin and inline config payloads. PATH must be explicitly selected for the owned executable tools, not copied from the parent.
- Data redirection is mandatory. `SQLiteCredentialTable` is in the global data database, and a fresh database can still import legacy `<global.data>/auth.json`. A new DB alone does not establish isolation. Runtime must inspect the run database and prove the credential table is empty and legacy credentials were not imported. No-auth-used does not mean no-file-access.
- Normal Windows CLI with `--standalone` is the candidate, spawning its private `serve --stdio --port 0`. Initial rendering does not require provider login or model execution. Fresh DB has no well-known origins, and the disabled model fetch/updater are source-grounded controls, not a no-network observation. `ensurePluginRuntime` package-relative module loading is not user-plugin discovery. A controlled Windows run or controlled Linux image is eligible. Normal POSIX `/etc/opencode` and macOS MDM discovery are not disabled by these controls. No macOS or all-platform claim is made.

**Later fixture staging:** after prerequisite validation, the owned live runner must create a unique ignored `.scratch/harness-host/run-<id>/`, all redirected directories, a working directory with no project config, and an evidence directory. Copy this fixture as `config/opencode/plugins/proceed-qualification/tui.ts`, using the documented discovered TUI-only layout. Write only a run-local `config/opencode/cli.json` with `plugins:[{package:"<absolute run plugin directory>",options:{evidence_directory:"<absolute run evidence directory>"}}]`. Validate this exact loading shape against pinned 2.2.5 before launch. Do not read or copy existing user configuration. Production imports no test fixture.

**MSYS candidate and stop rule:** discovery reports installed `script` 2.40.2 and Windows OpenCode 2.2.5 on PATH. Compatibility is UNPROVEN. Candidate arguments are `--command '<absolute OpenCode executable> --standalone' --return --quiet --flush --echo never --log-out <run transcript> --output-limit 1048576`. The pure builder quotes shell literals but does not prove MSYS path translation, console attachment or child I/O. Actual fixture setup plus rendered dialog title must precede each key write. Exercise Ctrl+G then Esc, Ctrl+G then Enter (Reject), Ctrl+G then search Respond/Enter followed by the separate prompt text `approved`/Enter. Check exact JSONL outcomes and dismissal without a result. Prompt/selection filtering and terminal-response handling are unqualified until tested. If MSYS cannot drive the real Windows console, stop with that failure. Compare a controlled Linux-image proof or bounded operator-assisted Windows observation before any new package/native ConPTY/Job Object adapter. Do not build one blindly or mask host-only variability.

**Live lifecycle work still required, owned by test engineering:** the current resource runner's root/PID cleanup cannot be assumed sufficient for Windows MSYS descendants or a private host server that outlives its parent. Complete one maintained setup/run/teardown lifecycle with ownership-aware descendant tracking, bounded termination and verified absence, without killing unknown processes. Persist progress/readiness and separate assertion/cleanup outcomes under the run root. DevOps recommendations are 300000 ms aggregate, 310000 ms outer command, 45000 ms reserved teardown, 60000 ms readiness and 15000 ms per interaction. Root cause is the shared fixture, not product behavior: `telegramFixture.getUpdates` blocks until an update at or after the requested offset exists, so once the wrong-sender update is consumed (cursor equals that update) the poller's next request uses `offset = wrongUpdate + 1`, which stays blocked until a later fixture update is enqueued. The checkpoint enqueues that later update only after this wait, so no completed batch with a greater offset can exist. Observed with a bounded native reproduction using the same fixture: cursor advanced to the wrong update, batches at offsets 2 and 3 only, poller waiting on offset 4. The runner's redacted classifier reports `UNCLASSIFIED` because its fixed signature still expects the now-passing keygen CLI failure; a bounded TAP re-run inside the pinned image identifies the single shared enrollment wait on both failing cases.

Consuming the wrong update is proven by the later press being reachable.

All sources below are pinned to official commit `9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21`:

- [TUI built-ins](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21/packages/tui/src/plugin/builtins.ts) lists 14 normal built-ins. Storybook is conditional on `OPENCODE_STORY`, absent from the fresh allowlist. `tests/support/harness-plugin-inventory.mjs` retains every normal import origin, including bundled Merman and Latex. Explicit and discovered copies of the same fixture are not two plugins. [Host resolution](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21/packages/plugin/src/host.ts) resolves the documented `tui` entry, through named exports when present or a conventional root file otherwise.

The fixture separately invokes the real read-only `context.client.plugin.list` and retains enumerated service IDs/origins/features. This distinguishes the two runtime inventories without requiring zero built-ins or rejecting the fixture's own service-side inventory description.

### Addendum to SE minimum checkpoint handoff - genuine product TUI next

The broker subset in the existing handoff is implemented and independently passes in its recorded scope. The next minimum production checkpoint, after combined review and coordination dispatch, is the smallest real product UI and adapter path that makes qualification possible. Do not require a positive product proof before implementing it. Do not implement broad groups 3/4 before mandatory authority/TLS/session qualification.

- **Production files:** new TUI-only entry and direct secure client under `src/harness/`, existing `src/harness/operator-key.js`, `src/http/local-client.js`, `src/mcp/server.js`, `src/mcp/execution.js`, `src/cli/args.js`, `src/cli/main.js`, and minimal package/build inclusion in `package.json` and `scripts/build.mjs` if required. Existing broker modules may change only for a demonstrated contract gap under the earlier bounded scope. This is a file-bound brief, not a private export/class mandate. SE reports its exact entry and source paths back to test engineering. A path/scope/dependency expansion returns through coordination.
- **Entry/packaging:** provide the pinned host's documented `tui` entry for the product package. SE selects either a conventional root `tui.js`/`tui.ts` included in package files or named `./tui` resolution to its source. Preserve CLI/portable entry behavior. Import only the supported SDK subpath in TUI-only code. No portable module may import the fixture or require host UI peers. No new runtime dependency, optional peer, SDK update, lock/version change or service decision RPC is authorized by this setup. Packaging implementation itself belongs to SE, not these tests.
- **Minimum behavior:** genuine product setup renders the enrollment fingerprint and explicit pending/enrolled state, reads only configured protected state/lock/operator-key paths and never owner/control/Telegram secrets, and uses the required direct TLS-PSK path. A client-local explicit setup action proposes enrollment only. Only current paired-chat confirmation activates it. The picker lists documented host sessions by title and exact ID. Selection is navigation only, followed by `data.session.get` verification and only that session's pending lookup. The question currently renders the broker's complete inert immutable presentation with a non-Approve default and a separate Respond prompt. Commands/arguments/RPC can open UI but cannot supply its answer. Capture each binding and fence picker/question/Respond callbacks on dismissal, replacement, deadline, disposal and Cleanup without cancelling durable work or imposing a global broker queue. The generic platform select fixture is not this implementation.
- **Metadata minimum:** wire the existing adapter opt-in `mcp --owner <owner_id> --harness-id <harness_id>` and capture only `params._meta["ai.opencode/sessionID"]` on BOTH preparation and creation. Forward the reviewed optional `harness_route`, preserve conflict/missing-metadata rules and the five model schemas, and carry `SESSION_BUSY` through the closed error vocabulary. Do not invent foreground-session, tool-argument or host-provided human metadata. Preserve shared owner registration and original waiting-call correlation, 45000 ms lease, 32000 ms wait turnover and separate 150000 ms startup budget.
- **Before positive product proof:** this production entry, protected loader, package inclusion and metadata forwarding must be present. Then test engineering binds public key sequences, actual SDK attacks and wire observations to it. Test-only transport success, source entry checks or direct authenticated broker decisions do not establish genuine product operator success. No captured callback, mocked host context or test-only production API may stand in for it.

### Qualification order, invariants and remaining facts

1. Preparation checks and combined affected review come first. SE implements the minimum product checkpoint. Test engineering composes its runtime inputs on the same branch. Platform primitives and unchanged broker 3/3/scheduling 20/20 evidence are reused by their recorded revisions and assertion scopes, never promoted to product proof.
2. In the next authorized contained run, establish complete inventory and embedded transport controls, then genuine product fingerprint display/matching fixture enrollment. Observe no requests or work approvals during setup. Do not confirm a substituted agent enrollment. Inspect fake-listener capture before redaction and keep synthetic operator keys outside package/state/config. Reserve teardown inside the existing aggregate, do not add seven new full deadlines outside it. Product 64 KiB, no redirects/proxy/downgrade, full protected-loader checks and cleanup aborts must additionally be tested on the actual product path, not inferred from the existing general client's larger response ceiling.
3. Open a real product request dialog with actual rendered keys before ordinary-API effects. Run admitted native reply and synthetic record controls, actual public RPC/helper attempts against the implemented surface, programmatic `keymap.dispatch` with approval-like arguments and direct public broker submissions with ordinary host Basic/owner/request authority. An unregistered guessed RPC returning 404 is not the entire attack proof. Obtain the actual exposed RPC/helper inventory from the implemented entry. Each attack must leave authoritative request/presentation pending and must not settle the local dialog. Without reopening or supplying a callback answer, genuine rendered selection must subsequently commit its captured binding over the authenticated operator wire. Exercise Esc, default Reject, explicit Approve and separate Respond `approved`. No native permission changes or automatic agent continuation.
4. Separately arm a transparent container-private MCP ingress/egress observer BEFORE actual session invocations. Do not add or rewrite `_meta`. On the real host, two equal-title/label sessions must share one adapter, remain independently pending before any selection, be selected by exact ID plus title and show only their own complete immutable question after verified lookup. Preserve captured bindings across tab changes and resolve in reverse order to their original waiting IDs without a global broker queue. Apply `assertOriginalCallRouting` to actual observed messages, not generated records. HTTP route fixtures and client-local dialog controls cannot qualify this path. Run direct and Code Mode variants separately for any claimed support.
5. Preserve the existing map: one pending slot and same-request replay (2.3/3.3), distinct concurrent sessions (2.3), first-valid Telegram/harness/expiry/cancel races and unchanged loser (3.4), inert late bindings/new same-session request (3.4/4.1), Respond always instructions (2.2/3.4), invocation cancellation distinct from durable `cancel_approval` and expiry (4.1), durable occupancy/recovery (3.3/3.5), callback/transport/timer cleanup and fresh reload (4.1). Current broker evidence covers only its reached subset. New shared assertions are not a claim that those remaining cases have run.

**Original-call feasibility fact:** pinned [tool/mcp.ts](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21/packages/core/src/tool/mcp.ts) passes `context.sessionID` to the registered MCP tool execution. [mcp/client.ts](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/9bbb8ce18c80f099e0e448775f59d965ead40a21/packages/core/src/mcp/client.ts) lines 426-435 places it in real `tools/call` metadata. That is representative source grounding, NOT runtime propagation evidence. The installed public client has session prompt and MCP configuration operations but no direct `mcp.callTool` method. Do not invent one or inject the desired metadata in a fixture. A feasible provider-backed route is two disposable real host sessions, a bounded `session.prompt` in each, registered real Proceed prepare/wait tool execution, and transparent observation of their actual shared MCP stream. A Code Mode claim needs its own registered Code Mode execution path, not a source assumption. A provider/model selection, isolated nonproduction access, a maximum permitted token/spend budget and permission to start it have not been supplied. Cost is therefore unknown. ### W3 bounded diagnostic correction - no runtime retry

Input HEAD `83badff8b18fab1ecdabaf669afd8d5e76b7e21a`, W3 executable `40b9c5fbbd9c8ddff82d023c15801bede7520778`, unchanged production `b51c5dfa2db925002f2d6e71c60a1fcb0a88e630` and base above. No production, dependency, normative artifact or archive change. No OpenCode, container, broker, TLS, network, provider or Telegram execution is authorized or performed here.

The actual failure to detect is loss of a typed embedded transport refusal before a blocked raw-capture export. Reuse the existing lifecycle and a single sanitized record, not a new framework or a full W3 retry. This is observability/proxy work, not the founder-visible genuine operator decision or proof of TLS compatibility. The prior W3 FAIL, all later UNRUN checks and the unused single full-product attempt remain unchanged.

- `assertRuntimeRoleOutcome` observes the actual caught error before the enrolled assertion can replace it. The fixture retains that projection through its catch. Native synthetic control proves `BROKER_INSTANCE_CONFLICT` plus a supplied `ECONNRESET` cause survive an `ERR_ASSERTION`, while a missing full-scan marker still refuses detailed export. This proves retention, NOT that either code occurred in the destroyed prior run.
- Sanitized schema: `schema_version:1`, fixed allowlisted `case` and `phase`, fixed `classification` (`CONTROL_FAILURE` or `FIRST_EXCHANGE_PASS`), integer `elapsed_ms` clamped to 0-100000, `chain` of at most four `{name,code}` entries and `runtime:{bun,node,openssl}`. Unknown names/codes/cases/phases become `REDACTED`. Runtime values accept bounded numeric release versions only, otherwise null. Only named own data properties are read. No message, stack, request, headers, configuration, environment, arbitrary fields, paths or authority values are serialized. No API-presence claim is treated as TLS-PSK support.
- Before creating `diagnostic-export.json`, the inner runner validates the exact schema and scans its serialized bytes against every known synthetic operator, owner, control and transport secret from the run. Failure handling no longer copies arbitrary `result.json` strings or progress around that gate. Transport outer verdicts use fixed failure labels rather than raw command/error strings. Container-private transcript/logs and bounded PTY capture stay internal until the existing owned teardown destroys the namespace. No failed raw capture crosses the boundary merely to obtain a diagnostic.

### Minimum implementation brief for software engineering

After the combined review and coordination dispatch, implement one exchange helper and its TUI parent lifecycle on this same branch. No product qualification pass is required before writing the implementation that makes qualification possible. No broad group 3/4 implementation is authorized by this brief.

| Files / boundary                                                                | Allowed implementation and constraints                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/harness/tui-plugin.js`, new application-owned modules under `src/harness/` | Keep protected configuration/key loading and genuine local dialog authority in the TUI closure. Replace its direct operator TLS invocation with a private child exchange. Keep the four options unchanged. Resolve the packaged helper relative to the installed package, not test selection or current directory. Private module structure and helper entry filename belong to implementation. Report the selected repo-relative helper entry in the handoff so the test runner can launch that real file directly.                                                                                                                                      |
| `src/http/local-client.js`, only if needed for remaining-budget reuse           | Reuse `createSecureLocalClient(...).request(method,path,body,{}, {signal})` and its existing protected lock, exact PSK profile and pre-HTTP complete-record reread. Preserve owner/control defaults and the broker wire. Existing `createOperatorLocalClient({port,lockFile,token})` fixes a fresh 10000 ms budget and 65536-byte cap. Calling it unchanged after spawn would restart the budget and is not sufficient. Carry only the remaining active-work budget into the existing secure exchange, always with the operator 65536-byte cap and min(1000 ms, remaining active work) TLS ceiling. How that reuse is factored is private implementation. |
| Unchanged production boundaries                                                 | No startup, broker, journal, enrollment, MCP, Telegram, package/dependency/version, public interface or service change. `src/broker/portable-server.js` remains the real endpoint. `src/harness/operator-key.js` remains the TUI protected loader, never imported to discover/read a key in the helper. Source is read-only during this test setup. Return an unavoidable out-of-brief change through coordination rather than expanding it.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

The parent resolves and identity-rechecks a trusted absolute genuine Node >=24 executable and package-relative helper. No shell, `fork`, Bun `execPath`, current-directory search, inherited `execArgv`, runtime injection variables, automatic install or new plugin option. Missing/unsupported/unverifiable Node gives the fixed nonsecret unavailable-channel explanation and preserves Telegram. A higher Node version is not a compatibility claim.

Spawn one child with a clean explicit OS-only environment, ignored stdin, bounded zero-output stdout/stderr tripwires, and private pipe slots 3 and 4. Parent sends `hello`, validates genuine Node `ready`, then hands off authority once in `exchange`. Keep slot 3 open until cleanup as a lifetime signal. No secret or operation body in argv/environment/files/output/diagnostics. The helper has no key-path option, listener, descendants, discovery, generic URL/header/proxy operation or owner/control route. Ordinary Shell/API launch without the separate operator credential remains unable to decide or activate enrollment. Parent PID and knowing the protocol are not authority. Same-account malware protection is not promised.

Use the design's exact strict length-prefixed UTF-8 JSON contract: 1-81920-byte frames, 98304-byte directional traffic limits, exact fields/order/IDs/version, no BOM/duplicate keys/trailing bytes, no arbitrary messages or raw error forwarding. Closed operations are `enrollments`, `pending`, `presentation`, `decisions`, mapped only to the four existing POST routes. Validate body/binding before a connection. Preserve broker refusal responses and role semantics, not a helper-specific HTTP remapping.

One parent monotonic 10000 ms budget starts before resolution/spawn. Child caps its entry timer by `deadline_unix_ms`. Reserve the last 1000 ms before protected/network work. Fence callbacks before abort, request termination immediately and escalate by 500 ms, verifying exit and closure by 1000 ms and within the original budget. Admit a response only after clean child exit/pipe closure and while its generation/interaction remains active. EOF/parent death, cancellation, dismissal/replacement, reload, expiry and Cleanup must not authorize a late next phase. Unconfirmed cleanup disables that generation. A lost committed response is uncertainty, followed by an explicit read-only refresh of the retained binding, never rollback or automatic decision replay.
